(11) a `percent`-family measure whose value prints no %, bps, x or 'percent' within 14 characters after it gets the scale note `no_unit: a ratio printed without its %, bps or x` and is served unverified; (12) `FORWARD_CUE_RE` (`forward_number_cue`): a v2 transcript `number` row whose `printed_label` or preceding clause (up to 80 chars after the last `.`, `?`, `;` or ` and `, with no past-actual cue between the forward cue and the value) carries a forward cue converts to `record_type='guidance'` (unless `claim_type` is `third_party`/`hypothetical`, which stays `number`) and gets `forward_number: a stated future figure, served as guidance` so it stays `unverified`, while past-actual statements ("grew by", "we have done", "was ... last year") and cues in an earlier sentence stay plain `number` rows; (13) `industry_figure_cue`: a v2 transcript `number` row whose measure starts with `industry_` / `NEW:industry_` / `NEW:market_`, whose label or clause names a market/industry/macro metric (`INDUSTRY_CLAUSE_RE`, skipping `"our"` / `"market share"`), or that cites a named agency source near the value (`INDUSTRY_SOURCE_RE`) gets `entity_scope='industry'` and `industry_figure: a market/industry figure, not the company's` (`unverified`); (14) `not_management_speaker`: a v2 transcript `number` row whose `speaker` has a non-management role, is moderator/analyst-labelled, or was introduced by the moderator (`_ANALYST_INTRO_RE` / `QUESTION_WE_HAVE_INTRO_RE` on the page or `obs_speaker.build_doc_context` during `--reparse`) gets `not_management: said by a participant, not the company` (`unverified`); (15) `apply_measure_conflicts`: after all page rows are built, v2 `number` rows sharing `(value_text, value, sentence)` keep one row per measure and mark every row in multi-measure groups `unverified` with `measure_conflict: one printed figure mapped to several measures`; (16) `RANGE_TAIL_RE` / `printed_range_high` now allow a scale/percent unit word between the low value and `'to'` (`Rs.1200 Crores to 1300 Crores`, `18 percent to 20 percent`, `5 million to 6 million`) while enforcing unit agreement across both ends; (17) `transcript_bound_kind` (`UPPER_BOUND_TAIL_RE`, `LOWER_BOUND_TAIL_RE_V2`): a v2 transcript `number` row whose label or own preceding clause ends with a bound cue (`more than`, `over`, `at least`, `upwards of`, `in excess of`, `less than`, `below`, `up to`, `at most`, `not more than`, `maximum of`) sets `value_kind='lower_bound'` or `'upper_bound'` and marks it `unverified` in `evaluate_status`; (18) `BY_CHANGE_RE` / `ADDED_CHANGE_RE` + `QUARTER_CHANGE_AFTER_RE`: a v2 transcript level row printed after `increased/decreased by` (allowing trailing approximation/currency words) or `added|net additions of|incremental|addition of` followed by a quarterly period cue (`during/in the quarter`, `this quarter`, `in Q[1-4]`, `sequentially`, `QoQ`) converts to `NEW:<root>_change` with `direction='up'` for additions; (19) `transcript_adjusted_cue` (`TRANSCRIPT_ADJUSTED_RE`): a v2 transcript `number` row whose label or own clause carries an adjusted-basis cue (`excluding`/`exclude`, `ex-<item>` excluding delivery/corporate terms like `ex-factory`, `adjusted`, `pre-Ind AS 116`, `like-for-like`, `on a comparable basis`, `pro forma`) sets `adjusted=1` so `evaluate_status` marks it `unverified`. Tests `reality_engine/tests/test_v2_guards.py`.
- **v2 transcript number guards and `--reparse` (T-241, 24 Nov 2026, Eagle).** Wolf's grade of the first fy27_v2 audit checkpoint (2,000 pages, v2e prompt) failed on numbers: 15 right of 29 (statements 30 of 31). Five script guards now run on every v2 transcript row (`transcript_v2`, or a reply carrying `claim_type`), code only, so the read-gate fingerprint stays `e95108f16e65`: (1) `negative_printed`: 'negative', 'loss of', 'minus', 'net loss' in the label or the 25 characters before the value negates a level row ('EBITDA stood at negative Rs27 crore' -> -27; never a measure named *loss* or a movement already signed); (2) `PERIOD_VALUE_RE`: a value that is a period label ('H1FY28', 'Q2', 'FY27') is dropped, numbers and guidance; (3) `is_cover_letter`: a page 1-2 with Scrip/Security Code, 'Sub:' and 'Dear Sir/Madam' gives no rows at all; (4) a `value_high` below the value is dropped ('20% or 10%'); (5) `printed_range_high`: with no `high_text`, the upper end printed right after the value ('$23 billion to $25 billion', '60% to 65%'; must exceed the low and be under 20x, and both ends share a unit: 'PAT rose by 11% to INR34.5 crores' is a growth and a level, never a range, a false range the first --reparse of 394_v2 caught) fills `value_high`, for guidance from its sentence first. `--reparse --baseline <db with page_reply> --out <copy> --stores ... [--only sel.json]` (`reparse_db`) rebuilds the selected transcript and annual-report pages' rows from the stored raw replies with today's parsing and guards, with no model call and no read gate; v2 guards apply when the reply's `page_reply.fingerprint` names the v2 prompt; `page_read` is updated; an annual-report page is rebuilt with the reader's own inputs (text layer, words, deck as-of/basis, the stored `page_ocr` text) and its statement lines then pass `apply_ar_statement_gate` and its verified number rows the T-242 AR row gates (next entry); deck pages are counted, not re-parsed (they also need words/OCR). Pages read before `page_reply` existed (fy27_v2 ckpt_0001) cannot be re-parsed. Forward ranges stored as number rows ('will be 18-20%') need the prompt, not a script: left for v2f. Wolf's grade of the second re-parse (seed 31337: 38/15/3/4 of 60; verified rows alone 20/4/0/1) added: (10) `word_fraction`: '14 and a half percent' is 14.5 (also 'and a quarter', 'three quarters'); a range's sign flips as a whole, both from hy4's `sign` field and from guard 1, and guard 5 reads the printed range on the absolute low, so '25 to 30 bps reduction' is -30..-25, never -25..+30; (14) `not_management_speaker`: a v2 transcript `number` row whose `speaker` has a non-management role, is moderator/analyst-labelled, or was introduced by the moderator (`_ANALYST_INTRO_RE` / `QUESTION_WE_HAVE_INTRO_RE` on the page or `obs_speaker.build_doc_context` during `--reparse`) gets `not_management: said by a participant, not the company` (`unverified`); Run it on the universe stores with `T228_STORE_ROOT=C:/RealityEngine/tg_build/full394` (pilot: 20 seeded stores, out `C:/RealityEngine/t242/events_pilot.db`). - **Order-filing fixes in `reality_engine/research/filing_events.py` (T-242, 24 Nov 2026).** Six script fixes from the 60-row independent grade (`D:/RealityEngine/t242/events_grade/events_grade.csv`, R/P/W/J = 21/23/12/4): (1) Customer parsing (`_annexure_awarders`, `_clean_party`, `order_fields`) prefers the Reg 30 annexure item `Name of the entity ...` (including two-column wrapped labels), cuts body-captured names at ` for `, ` amounting`, ` as per`, ` worth`, ` signif`, and rejects names >80 chars, money tokens (`Rs/INR/₹/worth/crore`), or generic descriptions (`our valued customer`, `a leading`, `customer`, `one of the ...`), storing `NULL` instead; (2) Cancellations and reductions (`_CANCEL_REDUCE_CUE`) retype to `order_amendment` with negative `amount_inr_cr` (with `_locate` print-checking `abs(target)`), while GST/DRC-07/assessment/demand/penalty/show-cause orders are vetoed via `_ORDER_VETO`; (3) `fund_raise` requires an issuance verb (`_RAISE_ISSUANCE`) and vetoes debt-servicing notices (redemption, call/put option, covenant, debenture trust deed, amendment of terms, interest payment) via `_RAISE_DEBT_VETO`, tagging multi-instrument proposals as `mixed`; (4) Results press releases (`_RESULTS_RELEASE`) emit no `order_win`, and quarterly/half-yearly order-inflow roll-ups (`_ORDER_ROLLUP`) are skipped; `capex_approval` and `fund_raise` on results releases (and `capex_approval` when financial results are present) require the amount in the same sentence as `approved`/`awarded`/`sanctioned` and never beside `revenue|EBITDA|PAT|profit|income` (dropping refinancing/delay false positives too); (5) Multi-item filings (`order_rows` reading all 4 loaded pages plus `_ITEM_LOA_AMOUNT` fallback and `_FILING_TOTAL`) emit one row per item (`seq = 1, 2, ...`) with `value_total` set to the printed filing total (`_fold_same_order` skips same-`doc_key` rows and `unique` includes `seq`); (6) Duplicate raise steps (`_raise_stage`, `_assign_raise_ids`) mark DLOF/addendum/EGM-notice filings as `proposed`, cluster `None`/`mixed` equity-family raises under one `raise_id`, and keep only the earliest row per `(symbol, instrument, amount, raise_stage)` as `verified`. Measured with `--no-model` on the 20 pilot stores (`C:/RealityEngine/t242/pilot_stores.txt`, `C:/RealityEngine/t242/fix_runs/events_fix.db` vs script baseline): `309 -> 280` total events (`order_win` `265 -> 245`, `order_amendment` `0 -> 10`, `capex_approval` `4 -> 1`, `capacity_commissioned` `1 -> 1`, `fund_raise` `39 -> 23`), verified `275 -> 262`, `need_model` `27 -> 10`; across the 60 graded rows (2 were model-sourced in `events_pilot.db`), 21/21 R rows kept (only `E07` updated its `raise_id` and `raise_primary` 1->0 when linked to the later QIP allotment), 12/12 W fixed (9 dropped, 3 retyped to negative `order_amendment`), 4/4 J fixed (2 results roll-ups dropped, `E06`/`E11` staged as `proposed` with `E06` demoted to `unverified`), and all 23 P rows addressed. Tests in `reality_engine/tests/test_filing_events.py` (61 passed with `test_no_control_chars.py`).
- **Order-filing round-2 fixes in `reality_engine/research/filing_events.py` (T-242, 24 Nov 2026).** Six script fixes from the 56-row independent grade on 20 new stores (`D:/RealityEngine/t242/events_grade_new20/grades.csv`, R/P/W/J = 31/17/8/0): (1) `capacity_commissioned` (`capacity_fields`, `_COMM_VERB`, `_is_stale_commissioning_clause`, `_pick_capacity_match`, `_plant`) requires a commissioning verb (`commissioned|commenced commercial operations/production|COD|started production|put to use|synchronised|commissioning of`) in the title, subject, or first substantive sentences (after cover-letter `PRESS RELEASE` skip), rejects `foundation stone|bhoomi pujan|groundbreaking|acquisition|acquired` clauses and clauses dated >90 days before `filing_date`, picks the unit capacity from the commissioning sentence itself (skipping host-facility `"at its 15 MMTPA ... Refinery"` and quoted project-name sizes, with explicit `"installed capacity addition"` fallback), adds `MMTPA`/`MWp`/`kWp`/`million pieces`, and normalises `plant` while never returning `'India'`; (2) `fund_raise` (`_raise_size`, `_raise_stage`, `raise_fields`, `_RAISE_OUTBOUND_VETO`, `_NCD_RX`) skips `Market Capitalisation` figures, vetoes outbound investments (`subscribe to` / `invest in` / `acquire stake in`), matches intervening adjectives in NCDs (`_NCD_RX` ranked ahead of `preferential` after stripping SEBI template lines), and classifies `'not exceeding'` board approvals as `raise_stage='approval'` (excluding `"prospectus and allotment of securities"`); (3) `customer` (`_ANNEXURE_AWARDER`, `_ANNEXURE_LABEL_STRIP`, `_FOR_ENTITY_HEAD`, `_clean_party`, `order_fields`) strips `|`, leading `— the`, trailing `Ref...` and row numbers, adds `bank` to `_FOR_ENTITY_HEAD` so `"National Bank for Agriculture..."` stays intact, keeps dotted abbreviations (`B.S.C`), and parses `'Name of the entity awarding the contract : X'`; (4) `order_amendment` (`_order_amendment_clause`, `_amendment_customer`) strips SEBI LODR `'amendments thereof'` boilerplate, gates on an amend/variation/cancel/reduce cue in the title/subject/opening three sentences, and extracts the customer on cancellations/reductions; (5) `capex_approval` (`_CAPEX_REVISED_VIS`) records prior cost and signed delta in `detail` for both `from A to B` and `revised to B vis-a-vis A` revisions, and `model_rows` routes `'annual revenue'` figures into `value_annual`; (6) `main()` reconfigures `sys.stdout`/`sys.stderr` with `errors='replace'` so `₹` in the summary never fails on a `cp1252` console. Measured with `--no-model` (`T228_STORE_ROOT=C:/RealityEngine/tg_build/full394`): on `new_stores.txt` (`C:/RealityEngine/t242/fix_runs/events_new20_fix.db`, `3,519` docs, `194` classified, `need_model` `36`), `177` events (`order_win` `136`, `order_amendment` `4`, `capex_approval` `8`, `capacity_commissioned` `13`, `fund_raise` `16`; `135` verified, `42` unverified, `0` quarantined), keeping `28/28` script R rows (`3` R rows and `1` W row were model-sourced in `events_new20.db`), clearing `7/7` script W rows (`6` dropped, `e77` fixed to `52.83 mw` `Barsingsar`), and recovering all `4` previously missed commissioning filings (`HPCL Chhara 5.0 mmtpa`, `HPCL Visakh RUF 3.55 mmtpa`, `Stove Kraft solar 4.0 mwp`, `Stove Kraft foundry 2.2 mn_pieces`); on `pilot_stores.txt` (`C:/RealityEngine/t242/fix_runs/events_pilot_fix2.db`, `3,680` docs, `303` classified, `need_model` `10`), `282` events (`order_win` `245`, `order_amendment` `10`, `capex_approval` `1`, `capacity_commissioned` `3`, `fund_raise` `23`; `264` verified, `18` unverified, `0` quarantined), keeping `21/21` R rows (`0` lost; `2` J rows were model-sourced). Tests in `reality_engine/tests/test_filing_events.py` (`87` passed with `test_no_control_chars.py` and `test_regress.py`).
- **Order-filing round-3 fixes in `reality_engine/research/filing_events.py` (T-242, 24 Nov 2026).** Seven script fixes from the fresh strict 60-row grade on 20 stores neither round was tuned on (`C:/RealityEngine/t242/events_grade_fresh3/grades.csv`, R/P/W/J = 41/9/9/1; by kind `order_win` 25/5/0/0, `fund_raise` 13/3/5/1, `order_amendment` 0/1/2/0, `capacity_commissioned` 2/0/1/0, `capex_approval` 1/0/1/0): (1) **per-kind gate** `VERIFIED_KINDS = frozenset({"order_win"})`: both status sites (`_assemble_row`'s last branch and `model_rows`' last branch) call `_gate_status`, so a row of any other kind is stored `unverified` with detail `kind_not_gated: <kind> awaits its own 60-row grade` (quarantine and parent-scope branches unchanged); a kind joins the set only after its own fresh strict 60-row pass. Same 20 stores before -> after: `fund_raise` verified 26 -> 0 (36 unverified), `capacity_commissioned` 4 -> 0, `capex_approval` 3 -> 0, `order_amendment` 3 -> 0, `order_win` 192 -> 193 verified; no non-order row is verified. (2) **`fund_raise` agreement veto** (`_RAISE_AGREEMENT_VETO`): credit facility / facility agreement / working-capital loan / supplemental-amendatory-addendum agreement / limit enhanced, reduced or surrendered / sanction letter / warrant forfeiture or lapse / investment or subscription in a subsidiary, JV or associate's shares, none of which is the company's issuance; a title naming the company's own QIP, NCD, preferential or rights issue (`_OWN_ISSUE_TITLE`) is exempt (#16, #19, #21, #43, #45 now gone). (3) **`raise_stage`** (`_ALLOTMENT_DONE`, `_APPROVAL_ONLY`): a board outcome that approves an issue is `approval` even when the annexure prints allotment in a future sense ('allotment of up to', 'date of allotment'); only a completed allotment ('issue and allotment of N shares', 'has allotted', 'allotment of N shares', or a 'post allotment of securities' outcome without an approval phrase) is `allotment` (#29, #46, #56, #60 now approval). (4) **`order_amendment`**: an L-1/lowest-bidder status is vetoed (`_LOWEST_BIDDER`) and a fresh letter of award / new order whose project name says 'loss reduction' is not an amendment, so no order event carries a negative amount (#52 and #58 are now positive `order_win` rows). (5) **`capacity_commissioned`**: a numeric printed date (`01.08.2018` / `2018-08-01`) more than 12 months before the filing date is restated history and vetoes the clause (`_is_stale_commissioning_clause`; a clause dated >90 days before the filing was already rejected) (#6 gone). (6) **`capex_approval`**: a stake acquisition ('acquire ... % stake', 'share purchase agreement', investment in a subsidiary/JV/associate) is vetoed (`_CAPEX_VETO`), so a target's project cost is never the company's capex (#51 gone). (7) **`customer`**: `_ANNEXURE_AWARDER` also reads `Name(s) of the entity awarding the order(s)/contract(s)` with the label and column header between label and value, `_ANNEXURE_LABEL_STRIP` drops a printed `/Contract(s)`, and `_clean_party` cuts an address tail, a `under the administrative control of` clause and a trailing university clause (all six missing customers filled: Jaypee Infratech, UP Jal Nigam (Urban), PGCIL, U.P. Jal Nigam (Rural), MPMMCC/BHU, and the MeitY society on #11); `_FROM_ENTITY` reads the prose form `from <Entity Name> (Limited|Ltd|Nigam|Corporation|Authority|...)`. `_fold_same_order` folds two same-customer order wins within 7 days at 0.5% value tolerance (was 1%), so two adjacent distinct KEC orders (#53) stay two events. Round-3 DBs: `C:/RealityEngine/t242/events_round3.db` (251 events) and `events_round3_new20.db` (176 events; `order_win` verified 103 -> 103); report `C:/RealityEngine/t242/events_round3_report.md`. Tests `test_t242_r3_fix1..7` + the fold counter-test.
- **Order-filing round-4 fixes (T-242, 24 Nov 2026, DeepSeek).** Seven script fixes from a second fresh strict 60-row grade on 25 stores neither earlier round was tuned on (`C:/RealityEngine/t242/events_grade_fresh4/grades.csv`, R/P/W/J = 46/11/2/1 = 5.0% W+J, exactly at the bar). (1) **Customer clean-up** (`_clean_party`): a trailing salutation (`Dear Sir/Madam`, `Respected Sir`) and a trailing city/address after a legal suffix (`Signatureglobal (India) Limited Mumbai` -> `... Limited`) are cut, and `_ANNEXURE_AWARDER` no longer starts a name inside a word (`[^:;]{0,140}?\s*(?P<cust>(?<!\w).+?)`). The **cause of the leading cut** was in `reality_engine/pilot3/order_wins.py` `_OC`: `(?:\s?\(?s\)?)?` after `Order/Contract` consumed the space plus the `S` of the next word (`uth Central Railway`); the plural is now attached or bracketed (`(?:\s*\(\s*s\s*\)|s)?`) and the trailing junk char is punctuation-only (`[^\w\s]?`), so `order(s)`, `orders`, `Order/Contract X` and `order (s) / contract (s)` all end at the value. `_FROM_ENTITY` takes one bracketed token (`GAIL (India) Limited`) and the body-name capture stops at `|`. (2) **`customer_desc`** (new nullable `event` column in `COLUMNS`/`SCHEMA`; `_ensure_event_columns` adds it to an older `--baseline` copy before a scoped merge): a value that is all lower case or starts `a|an|leading|key|domestic|customers|clients|one of|...` is a printed category, not an awarder — `_split_customer` moves it to `customer_desc` and leaves `customer` NULL, on script and model rows (`A hyperscale partner`, `Domestic Telecom Operator`, `Leading telecom infrastructure company`, `key customers in the renewable energy sector`). (3) **Period roll-up veto** (`_ORDER_ROLLUP`): `awarded orders worth X in FY24` and `orders received during the year` are aggregates, not an order (W #51, 900 cr); `order inflow/intake for FY` is read on the **title only** (`_ORDER_INFLOW_TITLE`) because a genuine order-win release (KEC, TRANSRAILL) quotes its own order inflow further down — vetoing the body dropped 10 real rows and was fixed before the final run. (4) **Title aggregate over a split table** (`_FILING_TOTAL` full-rupee branch + `order_fields`): when the title prints the aggregate and the table prints one item of it, the row is the printed aggregate (214.27 cr, not the 115.0194 TPSODL cell, W #38), confirmed against the body's "aggregate order value ... is ₹ 2,14,27,36,930/-". (5) **Cross-filing duplicate** (`_same_customer`): a customer printed once in full and once as its initialism (`TPSODL and TPWODL` vs `TP Southern Odisha Distribution Limited ...`) is the same customer, so the next-day press release (J #44) folds through the existing 7-day 0.5% path into the earlier row's `amend`; a token-subset rule was tried and removed because it folded graded R #7 (`Golden Temple`, 8.9 cr) into a 3.7083 cr row whose `value_annual` is 8.9. (6) Tests: six new cases in `test_filing_events.py` (one per step, from the quoted rows), 85 passed; `test_pilot3_order_wins.py` 7 passed. (7) Measured on the 25 fresh4 stores before -> after: 303 -> 301 events, `order_win` verified 133 (97 script + 36 model) -> 132 (96 + 36), one quarantined SUGSLLOYD row became verified (0.0047 -> 4.68 cr by the same aggregate rule), the two lost rows are W #51 and J #44, all 11 P customer rows are right and **no graded R row changed amount, customer or status**; on the 20 fresh3 stores the round-3 result stands (251 events, `order_win` verified 193, 0 rows lost vs `events_round3.db`).
- **Order-filing round-5 fixes (T-242, 24 Nov 2026, DeepSeek).** Eight script fixes on `fund_raise` from the third fresh strict 60-row grade on 45 stores neither earlier round was tuned on (`C:/RealityEngine/t242/events_grade_fundraise/grades.csv` + `pack.jsonl`, DeepSeek a8f57918: 30 R / 16 P / 10 W / 4 J). `VERIFIED_KINDS` and `order_win` logic untouched (`fund_raise` stays `unverified`). (1) **The step's own amount**: `raise_step_amount(text) -> (amount_cr, how)` (pure; the shared clause reader is `_raise_step_sizes`) stores THIS step's one printed total ("aggregating to Rs X", including an Indian-grouped figure whose currency mark the OCR dropped) or the shares it issued x the issue price — not a sum of angles (QIP 1,140.2161 -> 185.5 cr). (2) **Components are never added** (`_RAISE_COMPONENT` within 90 characters of the printed figure: 25%/75%, the remaining exercise price, an equivalent amount): the 25%/75% warrant receipts are not added (14.57 -> 9.92, 12.25 -> 7.0, 7.0 -> 4.0 cr) and a part conversion is 3,52,941 x 425 = 15.0, not the earlier 500 cr warrant allotment. (3) **Earlier steps and capital are cut at the cue, not summed** (`_RAISE_PRIOR_STEP`: `had allotted`, an earlier/previous issue, `in the FY 2014-15`; `_RAISE_CAPITAL`: paid-up capital before/after the issue, pre/post-issue): 850 -> 500 cr enabling resolution, 1,060.283 -> 1,000 cr QIP. (4) **Vetoes**: `_RAISE_SUBSIDIARY_VETO` (a subsidiary "has allotted" its own rights shares), `_RAISE_ADVISORY_VETO` (an exchange advisory or lock-in letter), and `_RAISE_GENERIC_CUE` without `_RAISE_SECURITY` ("fund raising through debt", nothing named) drop #19, #21 and #43. (5) **Stage map** (`_raise_stage`; `_ALLOTMENT_DONE`, `_RAISE_NOTICE`, `_RAISE_NOTICE_DONE`, `_RAISE_DRAFT_ONLY`, `_RAISE_IN_PRINCIPLE`, `_RAISE_LISTING`, `_RAISE_COMPLETION`): "allotted 300 NCDs" / "considered and approved the allotment of 1,19,99,847 Equity Shares" = `allotment`; a postal-ballot or EGM notice subject line = `proposed` (a board outcome that merely lists the notice among its resolutions approved the issue); "approved the draft Postal Ballot Notice" alone = `proposed`; an exchange in-principle approval = `approval` (not `listing`); `intimation` is gone. 13/13 stage rows right. (6) **Duplicates** (`_fold_same_raise`, called in `build` after `_fold_same_order`): a press release, errata or second letter repeating a step is one event — same company, amount within 0.5% or the same printed share count, within 10 days — and the repeat names the kept row in `amend` (#9, #29, #48, #60); two same-day letters about one step keep the more advanced stage, while a notice and a later stage on a later day are never folded. (7) Tests: six new cases in `test_filing_events.py` (one per step) plus a bundled-instruments counter-test; `test_stage_c_ballot_is_not_an_allotment` now expects `proposed`; 104 passed (86 before). (8) Measured with the round-4 cache (0 new model requests): on the 25 fresh4 stores 301 -> 290 events, `fund_raise` 86 -> 75 rows (all `unverified`), `amend` 27 -> 63, `order_win` verified 132 (96 script + 36 model) unchanged; on the 20 fresh3 stores 251 -> 249 events, `fund_raise` 36 -> 34, `amend` 30 -> 39, `order_win` verified 193 unchanged; across the 60 graded rows 30/30 R unchanged (amount, stage, status), 7/7 amount W fixed, 3/3 not-an-issuance W gone, 4/4 J folded with an `amend` link, 13/13 stage P right. DBs `.tmp/events_r5_fresh4.db` / `.tmp/events_r5_fresh3.db` (worker worktree, not installed): the controller still has to copy them to `C:/RealityEngine/t242/` and repoint `load_observations.EVENTS_PRIORITY` (still the round-4 DBs). Ceiling: a filing that prints only a 75% balance and no percentage keeps the printed balance (graded #22/#41/#45), and a repeat whose amount was read differently stays two rows.
- **Order-filing round-6 fixes (T-242, 25 Nov 2026, DeepSeek).** Three `order_win` causes from a surprise strict audit of 60 **verified** rows on the round-5 DBs (`C:/RealityEngine/t242/audit_orderwin_r5/`, DeepSeek b87f8d84: 52 R / 0 P / 8 W / 0 J = 13% wrong against the 5% bar); no `fund_raise` logic and no `VERIFIED_KINDS` change. (1) **L1-only is not an order yet**: `_AWARD_RECEIVED` (a receive/award verb + LoA/LOI/work order/purchase order/order/contract within 80 characters; round 8 tightens this to the verb beside the noun) and `_l1_only` (title/subject/first 600 characters say lowest bidder / L-1 / L1 status / "emerged as L1" and no award is received; round 8 reads the annexure past 2,600 characters), applied by a new `_finish_scope` at both status sites (`_assemble_row` and `model_rows`): the printed value stays, `status='unverified'` and `why='l1_only'` (detail names the rule; round 8 keeps an L1-only letter out of `order_win` entirely and the stamp stays as the defence for a row that slips through). A received LoA wins even when L1 is also mentioned (audit #26 Kolkata Municipal Corporation, fresh3 #28 KMEW "emerged as the lowest bidder"). 13 fresh3 verified rows unverified, all EMSLIMITED (6.7% of 193); 0 on fresh4. (2) **A subsidiary's order keeps its status and is labelled** (`_subsidiary_name`; `_SUB_AFTER_NAME` for "<Name>, a wholly owned subsidiary of the Company, has received"/"<Name> (wholly owned subsidiary)", `_SUB_BEFORE_NAME` for "our subsidiary <Name>"/"material subsidiary, <Name>"/"subsidiary - <Name>"/"US-based subsidiary <Name>"/"subsidiary of <Parent>, <Name>"; the printed "subsdiary" typo is accepted; candidates are trimmed at verbs/salutations/sentence ends and rejected for articles, qualifier-only or possessive parents, all-caps boilerplate, digits and >3 words without a company suffix): `blank_row` sets `entity_scope='subsidiary'` and `_finish_scope` stores the printed name in `detail` as `subsidiary: <name>` (`detail` is free text, not JSON). A filing that says only "a subsidiary"/"received by subsidiary company" keeps `company` (Wolf, 25 Nov); a third party's subsidiary is the customer, not the issuer's scope (`TenX Realty Limited, (a step-down subsidiary of Raymond Limited)`). 67 fresh4 + 9 fresh3 rows labelled, 0 status/amount moves. (3) **A press-release headline is not a customer**: `_HEADLINE_CUSTOMER` (headline verb or month + day) and `_HEADLINE_PLACE` (all-caps place + date) reject a candidate in `_clean_party`, so "BSNL to Power India's Telecom Future BANGALORE - January 01" is dropped and the existing body-name fallback returns "Bharat Sanchar Nigam Limited (BSNL)". Measured into `.tmp/events_r6_fresh4.db` / `.tmp/events_r6_fresh3.db` (cache copy, 0 new model requests): all 8 W rows fixed as above (#54 folds into the verified 94.3513 cr BSNL row and is linked in `amend`); 52/52 + 46/46 + 41/41 graded R rows unchanged in status and amount (the only non-R change: one EMSLIMITED L-1 P row, `events_grade_fresh3` #23, now `l1_only`); `order_win` verified fresh4 132 -> 132, fresh3 193 -> 179; 107 tests pass. Deliberate ceiling: the scope test is document-level, so an order the issuer received *from* its own subsidiary would also be labelled `subsidiary`.
- **Order-win gate withdrawn (T-242, 25 Nov 2026, Eagle).** `filing_events.VERIFIED_KINDS` is now EMPTY: `order_win` failed its strict grade on 40 untuned stores (fresh5, round 6: 39 R / 5 P / 14 W / 2 J; customers wrong, truncated or garbled, missed subsidiaries, JV gross values, past orders, quarterly roll-ups, repeats). Every event row is `unverified` until a kind passes a fresh-store grade. Event DBs built before this change still carry `verified` order wins: re-run filing_events before any store load. - **Order-filing round-7 fixes (T-242, 25 Nov 2026).** Structural `order_win` rules in `reality_engine/research/filing_events.py` for the fresh5 round-6 audit (39 R / 5 P / 14 W / 2 J); `VERIFIED_KINDS` stays empty and `fund_raise` logic is untouched. (1) **Customer**: `order_fields` prefers annexure awarders over `loose_order_v1` title guesses; `_ANNEXURE_AWARDER` stops before `Whether the acquisitions` / `Objects and effects` / `Indicative time period` / `Total cost`, `_ANNEXURE_TARGET_AWARDER` falls back to `Name of the target entity ...`, and `_ANNEXURE_LABEL_STRIP` drops `/Redevelopment(s)`, `/Letter of Intent|Acceptance|Award`, and garbled `con...t(s)` labels; `_strip_party_junk` strips leading/trailing bullets (`•●▪✓✔◦·*`), punctuation and single-letter OCR prefixes, `_clean_party` rejects date, month-year and period strings (`_DATE_OR_PERIOD_CUSTOMER`: `<Month> <YYYY>( to <Month> <YYYY>)?`, `Q1 FY26`, `FY 2025-26`, numeric dates), single generic words (`_SINGLE_GENERIC_CUSTOMERS`), `our`/`its`/`their`/`the Company's` prefixes (`_OWN_PARTY_PREFIX`), bare countries/regions (`_COUNTRIES_AND_REGIONS`), `<Day> <Month>` datelines (`_HEADLINE_CUSTOMER`, `_HEADLINE_PLACE`), and leading table-header words (`_HEADER_PARTY_PREFIX`), while `_BAD_CUSTOMER` narrows `promoter` to `whether the promoter`/`promoter/group` (keeping `Yucca Promoters LLP`). `_split_customer` and `_finish_scope` store `customer = NULL` and append `customer not disclosed` to `detail` on `'Not disclosed'` / `'Confidential'` / `'withheld'` (`_UNDISCLOSED_CUSTOMER`) across script and model rows. (2) **Subsidiary**: `_subsidiary_name` captures quoted names (`_SUB_AFTER_NAME`/`_SUB_BEFORE_NAME`), strips `(formerly known as ...)` before `_SUB_JUNK`, adds `GmbH` to `_SUB_SUFFIX`, extracts multiple comma/`and`-separated names for `subsidiaries` joined by `'; '`, accepts parent-named clauses followed by an active win verb (while still rejecting customer-side third-party subsidiaries), and rejects digits, capacity/unit tokens (`_SUB_UNIT_TOKEN`: `MW`/`MWp`/`DC`/`AC`/..., fixing `'MW DC'`), bare countries, and single-word non-suffix tokens. (3) **Value**: `_consortium_share` matches full-rupee share spans (`_CONSORTIUM_AMOUNT_FULL`) and issuer-gated named shares (`_issuer_tokens`, `_matches_issuer`), storing the company's share and noting the gross value in `detail`; `_strip_past_order_values` removes delivered/executed/earlier/previous order clauses/phrases before value extraction; `_ORDER_ROLLUP` vetoes `orders worth/aggregating to ... in Q2 FY ...` / `in the <Month-Month> quarter`. (4) **Duplicates & builder**: `_fold_same_order` folds a press/media release within 10 days of an order at 0.5% value tolerance when customers do not conflict (treating `Not disclosed`/`Confidential` as unnamed, and never folding a valued press release into an empty prior row); `_finish_scope` stamps `l1_only` and `build()`'s `covered` replaces empty script rows with valid model rows even when `VERIFIED_KINDS` is empty. Measured on copied caches (0 new model requests; `.tmp/events_r7d_fresh{5,4,3}.db`): **21/21 fresh5 W/P/J fixed**; date/period rejection cleared 3 `PREMIERENE` rows, bullet/punctuation stripping cleaned 3 `fresh5` rows + 1 `fresh4` row, and undisclosed handling moved 48 `fresh5` rows (`VEERHEALTH` 30, `QPOWER` 15, `HIGHNESS` 3) to `NULL` + `customer not disclosed` in `detail` (+1 `QPOWER` side-effect); graded R unchanged 25/25 `fresh3`, 51/52 `audit_orderwin_r5` and 45/46 `events_grade_fresh4` (`CAPACITE e8953bba` -> 40% JV share `153.888` cr, computed `unverified`), while on `fresh5` 2 `VEERHEALTH` press releases folded into previous-day Reg 30 filings and the 6 undisclosed-customer R rows moved to `NULL` + `customer not disclosed`; set diffs `fresh4` 11/199 (5.5%) and `fresh3` 3/209 (1.4%), both under the 15% stop bar; 114 tests pass in `test_filing_events.py`. Detail in `docs/research/T-231_filing_events.md` section 3f.
- **Order-filing round-8 fixes (T-242, 25 Nov 2026, DeepSeek).** Structural `order_win` kind vetoes plus the remaining scope/value/customer rules from the gate grade on 40 never-seen stores (`C:/RealityEngine/t242/audit_orderwin_fresh6/`, built with round-6 code: 31 R / 4 P / 23 W / 2 J; rebuilt with round 7, 22 of the 25 W+J failures stood). `VERIFIED_KINDS` stays empty; no rule is keyed on a company name. (1) **Kind gate** (`_not_an_order` used by `classify`, `order_rows` and `order_fields`; `_AUTHORITY_ORDER_STRONG`, `_AUTHORITY_OFFICE`, `_AWARD_NOUN`, `_REGULATOR_APPROVAL`, `_BOARD_OR_APPOINTMENT`, `_AWARD_QUOTED`, `_SELF_SETUP`, `_self_setup_loi`, `_CAPACITY_TOKEN`): the company (or a named subsidiary) must receive/be awarded a contract, LoA, LoI, work or purchase order from a counter-party. Vetoed — an order an authority issues TO the company (tax/compounding/assessment/demand/penalty/refund/remand/warning/acceptance-of-reply beside `order`, or `from the Office of the Commissioner/Inspector/Superintendent` without an award noun, or `SEBI|IRDAI|RBI|tribunal|court ... issued/passed/warned ... order`), a regulator's approval to launch a product, a board-meeting outcome or appointment unless it quotes an award (win verb + order noun + value/`worth`/`from <Name>`), and an LOI/LoA to set up the company's own outlet/dealership/showroom (a capacity project such as a 250 MW solar plant stays an order). `_l1_only` reads the annexure past 600 characters and `_AWARD_RECEIVED` now needs the award verb beside the noun, so the table label "Name of the entity awarding the order(s)" is never an award; an L1-only letter is no longer an `order_win` row. Counter-tests keep a real LoA "received from \<Authority\>", a GST-registered customer and "excluding GST" values. (2) **Scope** (`_SUB_AFTER_NAME` "which is", `_SUB_BEFORE_NAME` "of the Company, M/s"/"i.e.", `M/s` stripping, Latin-1 names, `_SUB_SUFFIX` AB/AS/Oy/SA, `_SUB_LEGAL_LEAD`, `_SUB_STOP`, `_JOINT_RECEIVER`, `_JOINT_SHARE`, `_names_subsidiary`, `blank_row`, `_finish_scope`): named-subsidiary forms print on both sides ("<Name> which is Material Subsidiary of the Company", "Material Subsidiary of the Company, M/s <Name>", "our subsidiary Company M/s. <Name>", "subsidiary Company i.e. <Name>"); a company AND a subsidiary awarded together is `entity_scope='joint'`, and when the filing splits the amounts ("Company ... Rs 300 cr and NPC ... Rs 700 cr respectively") the event is the company's own share with `value_total` the group total; a subsidiary's name is never the customer. (3) **Value** (`_not_order_size`, `_NOT_ORDER_SIZE_BEFORE/NEAR/AFTER`, `_ORDER_SIZE_SENTENCE`, `_FOREIGN_VALUE`, `_foreign_value`): an amount beside a GDV/project-cost/order-book/target/own-investment label is not the order size but must be adjacent (a genuine `Rs 534 crore` next to an "order intake" headline stays; a `Capex/Opex` note after a contract value does not disqualify it), a free-form "approximately INR 400 crore" sentence is readable, and a non-INR/non-USD value stays in `value_text` as printed with a detail note (never converted without a printed rate). (4) **Customer** (`_strip_unbalanced_brackets`, OCR hyphen join in `_strip_party_junk`, "supplied to the \<X\> consortium" in `order_fields`, unspaced hyphens allowed in `_HEADLINE_PLACE`): stray edge brackets are stripped, "Hous- ing" joins to "Housing", "BHEL- TRSL" to "BHEL-TRSL", a `<X> consortium` awarder is read, and a hyphenated company name is no longer a dateline. (5) **Duplicates** (`_fold_same_order`): an unvalued twin cover letter with the same normalised title and date folds into the valued twin, which transfers its amount/value_text to the kept (earlier) row. Measured with copied caches into `.tmp/r8_fresh{6,5,4,3}.db` (worktree `20261125T112919Z-fc9779c7`, 0 new model requests): every fresh6 W+J row fixed (16 gone, 9 in place, #19 carries the #52 USD 3.4 mn and #52 is gone; the 4 P rows also get their printed customer/value text); 193 graded R rows checked across the five sets and none worse (fresh6 #52 folded into #19; fresh4 #40 lost a stray ")"); fresh6 278 -> 257 rows, fresh5 258 -> 256, fresh4 198 -> 197, fresh3 209 -> 200 (4.3%, under the 10% stop); tests 138 passed. Detail `docs/research/T-231_filing_events.md` 3g.
- **Order-filing round-7 fixes (T-242, 25 Nov 2026, DeepSeek).** Structural `fund_raise` rules from a fresh strict 60-row grade on 40 untuned stores (`C:/RealityEngine/t242/events_grade_fundraise_fresh5/`, fresh5: 31 R / 7 P / 22 W / 0 J); no `order_win` logic and no `VERIFIED_KINDS` change (`fund_raise` stays `unverified`). (1) **Issuer rule** (`_RAISE_FOREIGN_ISSUE_VETO`, read on title + first two pages): a subscription to another entity's rights issue, an investment in or acquisition of another company's shares (cash or swap), a subsidiary's/associate's own allotment (also when the security is allotted TO the listed company), a loan granted to a subsidiary, a sale/divestment paid in another company's paper and "right to invest"/"co-invest"/"invested ... in <Name> Limited" are not the listed company's raise. (2) **Step amount** (`_raise_size`, `_raise_count_size`, `_raise_step_sizes`): the printed total of THIS step ("aggregating to", "total aggregating to", "issue size", "for an aggregate consideration of"), else the securities this filing allots/converts x the issue price of the security being allotted or converted. The green shoe inside "up to X including", a premium/base issue/cash or balance consideration/partly-paid tranche (`_RAISE_COMPONENT_BEFORE` reads the prefix), an earlier step ("pertaining to the allotment of", "till date", "after this allotment", "out of the balance"), a fallen-away allottee ("became ineligible") and a 25%/75% balance receipt (`_RAISE_PRIOR_STEP`, `_RAISE_NOT_STEP`, `_RAISE_BALANCE_CLAUSE`) never add — round 7b corrects the last one: a warrant CONVERSION stores the balance the letter prints, see the round-7b entry below. A converted security is priced itself (1,45,010 CCDs x Rs 1,00,000, never the 10,32,09,960 post-split shares x their conversion price; `_raise_instrument`, `_instrument_price`); the subject line's own allotment count wins; a size belongs to the row's instrument (`_raise_family_ok`: a QIP notice never takes an NCD resolution's amount, but a filing approving two instruments still sums them). (3) **Stage** (`_raise_stage`): a board recommendation to seek members' approval (`_RAISE_RECOMMEND`) and an AGM resolution / the AGM's own "for raising of funds" business (`_RAISE_AGM_AGENDA`) are `proposed`; a proposed "issue and allotment of N warrants" item is no longer a completed allotment (`_ALLOTMENT_DONE` now needs "considered and approved the allotment of N"); a press release that says the issue was successfully concluded/closed is `allotment` (`_RAISE_CONCLUDED`). Measured (fresh5 `.tmp/events_r7_fresh5.db`, cache copied, 0 new model requests): all 60 graded rows right (31 R unchanged; 15 W amounts fixed, 7 W vetoed, 6 P stages fixed; #45/#60 gone because the QIP prints no rupee size), verified `order_win` 215 unchanged (195 script + 20 model), fresh4 132 and fresh3 179 (fresh3 rebuilt `model_requests` 0). Tests `test_t242_r7_raise_step_amount_shapes`, `test_t242_r7_not_the_listed_company_as_issuer`, `test_t242_r7_raise_stage_shapes`; 121 passed in `test_filing_events.py`.
- **Order-filing round-7b fixes (T-242, 25 Nov 2026, DeepSeek).** The nine graded R rows round 7 broke on the round-5 grade set (`C:/RealityEngine/t242/events_grade_fundraise/`, stores fresh4/fresh3) are restored with no fresh5 fix lost; no `order_win` logic and no `VERIFIED_KINDS` change (`fund_raise` stays `unverified`). (1) **The company's own priced share issue is the raise** (`_OWN_PRICED_ISSUE`, `_OWN_ISSUE_RAISE`): the foreign-issuer veto is skipped when the clause that produced the size is the listed company's own priced issuance ("issuance of up to 19,99,996 equity shares of the Company ... at a price of Rs. 270 per equity share", or an NCD issue), the outbound veto skips the same shape, and `_RAISE_OTHER_DEAL` no longer vetoes that clause, so a swap paying another company's sellers in the company's own shares (#18, #24, #50) and a board that also approves selling an investment (#3) raise, while an unpriced swap tranche inside a cash raise is still not the step (fresh5 #49). (being remaining 75% of the issue price per warrant)" is the step (#17), else the count x the printed per-unit balance ("Post receipt of balance amount at the rate of Rs. 48.75 per warrant", #22, #41, #45), never a computed 100% from the issue price. (3) **Template cells and page-3 sizes**: a SEBI cell printing "Up to 48,134 Equity Shares of the Company ... at a price of INR 1454.30" without a raise verb is read (#18), and `classify(..., text=...)` sees a size printed past page 2 (#47 KPIGREEN). - **Model-first `order_win` and `fund_raise` with deterministic script checks (T-242, 26 Nov 2026).** `order_win` and `fund_raise` in `reality_engine/research/filing_events.py` switched from script-only rules to model-first reading via `hy4-flash-free` (`EVENT_PROMPT_V1`, `prompt_fp=546696b94a21` stored in `run_meta`, sending up to `MAX_MODEL_CHARS=20000` across pages 1..4) plus deterministic script verification, while `capex_approval`, `capacity_commissioned`, and `order_amendment` remain script-first. Candidate selection (`_order_win_candidate`, `_fund_raise_candidate`) filters non-raise noise via `_HARD_RAISE_VETO`. `model_rows` checks every extracted amount deterministically (`_parse_printed_amount` via `_find_printed_span`: verbatim substring on pages 0..4, Indian-comma 10x model digit-slip recovery from the actual printed digits, OCR `¥`->`₹` beside `crore`/`lakh`, spaced decimals like `43. 50`, `(In ₹ crore)` table cell headers, while ignoring non-monetary capacity quantities and per-share prices and never summing `board_limit`/`threshold`/`total_issue`; unprinted spans quarantine with `amount_inr_cr=None, amount_usd_mn=None, value_text=None`), validates `entity_scope` (`_awardee_printed`, `_is_listed_company_name` full normalized name match), prefers quantified raises when folding duplicate twins (`_fold_same_raise`), logs non-events (`is_event=false`, `bonus`, `l1_only`, `cancelled`, `withdrawn`) to a new `skip_log` table, and cross-checks model amounts against script parses (`_script_cross_check`, marking mismatches `unverified`). `run_model` honours shared 429 `Retry-After` cooldowns, caches by `(doc_key, prompt_fp)`, and halts if HTTP errors exceed `ERROR_STOP_SHARE=0.05`. `VERIFIED_KINDS` stays empty. Measured into `C:/RealityEngine/t242/model_v1/events_fresh{5,6,7}.db` (`2,362` live `hy4` requests across 120 stores + 2-store smoke test, `0` HTTP errors, `0` unprinted stored `value_text` excl. `_fold_same_order` twin copies): quarantined share `0.52%`/`2.01%`/`2.28%`; `W+J` resolution `16/16` (`orderwin_fresh5`), `24/25` truly fixed (`25/25` non-verified `orderwin_fresh6`), `15/15` (`orderwin_fresh7`), `22/22` (`fundraise_fresh5`), `16/16` (`fundraise_fresh7`); `R` unchanged: `36/39` (`92.3%`, `39/39` w/ `amend` on `orderwin_fresh5`), `26/31` row-level (`83.9%`, `30/31` doc-total sum on `orderwin_fresh6`), `34/35` (`97.1%` on `orderwin_fresh7`), `27/31` (`87.1%` on `fundraise_fresh5`), `24/31` row-level (`77.4%`, `27/31 = 87.1%` w/ `amend` on `fundraise_fresh7`) — `fund_raise` stops below `90%` per set chiefly because `4` `R` warrant/share allotments only print `shares x price` rather than a full rupee total on pages 1..4. Tests in `reality_engine/tests/test_filing_events_model.py` (`26` tests), `test_filing_events.py`, `test_load_observations_gate.py`, `test_regress.py`; detail in `docs/research/T-231_filing_events.md` section 9.
- **Mix segment guard in `v2/observations.py` (T-241, 24 Nov 2026).** Code-only guard in the number
  path of `build_observations` (no prompt text touched; transcript v2 `read_gate` fingerprint stays `e95108f16e65`):
  after dimension canonicalisation, any number row whose measure matches `MIX_MEASURE_RE` (`revenue.mix`, `mix.share`,
  `volume.mix`, or ending in `_mix` / `_share` excluding `_per_share`; an order-book measure is NOT matched, because a company's total order book needs no part -- Wolf's review caught KPGEL's total order book turned unverified) with
  `segment`, `product`, `geography` and `customer` all empty extracts the named part from `printed_label` via
  `mix_part()` (stripping boilerplate verbs, prepositions, articles and trailing punctuation, and rejecting stop-word-only
  or >6-word results) into `row['segment']` when non-empty; otherwise `mix_without_segment: a share row must name its part`
  is appended to `status_reason` so the row becomes `unverified` (and `apply_parts_sum` does not override it). Measured
  on a copy of `D:/RealityEngine/t241/prompt_ab/ab_v2e.db` (386 number rows): 38 rows match `MIX_MEASURE_RE`, 26 already
  carry a dimension, and of the 12 with all four dims empty, 1 receives `segment` from `mix_part` and 11 get
  `mix_without_segment` (4 turning `unverified` from `verified`, 7 already `unverified`). Tests in
  `reality_engine/tests/test_transcript_prompt_v2.py`: `test_mix_part_extracts_named_parts_and_rejects_empty_labels`,
  `test_mix_guard_sets_segment_from_label_when_dims_empty`, `test_mix_guard_marks_mix_without_segment_unverified`,
  `test_mix_guard_leaves_row_with_customer_or_non_mix_measure_untouched`.
- **Guidance scale in `v2/observations.py` (T-241, 24 Nov 2026).** `guidance_observation` in `reality_engine/v2/observations.py` previously left `_record_row`'s default `scale=1.0` and `unit_family=""` because `build_observations` returns early for `statement`/`guidance` before running the number-row scale block. Both paths now share `_apply_scale` (`printed_scale`, `infer_unit_family`, `money_scale`, `resolve_mt_scale`, `apply_per_unit_scale`), and `evaluate_guidance` pops `_scale_notes`, so guidance rows apply the exact same scale rules as number rows (`Crores` -> `1e7`, `lakh crore` -> `1e12`, `crore liters` -> `1e7` with `unit_family='count'`, `%` -> `1.0`) without touching any prompt template. Tests in `reality_engine/tests/test_observations.py`.
- **T-232: minimal deck stores for the non-91 Telegram companies (21 Nov 2026).** The deck-observation reader
  (`v2/observations.py`, T-230) reads `company`, `doc`, `doc_page` and `routes.csv` beside a store, and nothing
  else; a Telegram-list company the 91-store build does not cover gets exactly that from its investor
  presentations:
  ```
  python -m reality_engine.scripts.tg_measure_build --root D:/RealityEngine/tg_build/decks403 \
      --universe D:/RealityEngine/tg_build/decks403/universe.csv --doc-types deck --parts registry,pages
  ```
  `--parts` names recipe parts in build order (default: all 13, the T-214 recipe unchanged). With `--doc-types
  deck` the run is a deck build: store folders are `dNNN` (the T-214 recipe keeps `cNN`), it reads no T-214
  manifest and writes no T-214 accept copy; the company is resolved by the universe's `isin` column (36 of the
  ticket's 560 `symbol` cells are BSE codes, not symbols), and the per-company manifest is written from
  `deck_figures.deck_sources` for the company's whole ISIN history since 2024-04-01 - "Intimation" letters skipped
  (BSE files "Analyst / Investor Meet - Intimation" under the presentation category), one row per sha256,
  `local_path` re-rooted with `config.resolve_stored_path`. `make_profile` gets `--raw-root D:/RealityEngine/raw`:
  the PDFs stay on Drive and are fetched only into the RawStore cache while read, so a store keeps no PDF.
  `docs_collect --doc-types deck` prefetches no other type (a `concall_audio_link` PDF is read only when
  transcripts are asked for), and the route pass also gets `--doc-types deck`. The measure CSV keeps its T-214
  columns: `raw_ok_files`/`raw_ok_mb` are the decks fetched and `store_mb` the store on disk; the run prints both
  totals and the free space and stops before a company below `--min-free-gb` (default 50).
  Verified against the stores (21 Nov): the ticket's 560 ISINs are all in `master_companies`, 394 have a deck since
  2024-04-01 and 166 have none. `deck_sources` has 3,497 `ok` rows since that date (16.8 GB): 54 are "Intimation"
  letters and 39 repeat another row's sha256 -> 3,404 decks (16.4 GB), 0 held locally and all 3,404 in the Drive
  manifest (`raw_to_drive.db` holds 25,844 rows under `decks/`), so no deck is missing. The build's universe is the
  ticket CSV filtered to those 394 ISINs (an `isin,symbol` CSV; a company with no deck would only build an empty
  store). `deck_sources` stops at 2026-10-25; checked 21 Nov, 291 later "Investor Presentation" BSE filings for 274
  of the 560 companies are not fetched in this ticket (the scout's 273 for 258 had already grown).
  Pilot, 10 companies seeded from the ticket CSV (first ten with a deck: RAILTEL, SOLEX, GPTINFRA, OMPOWER,
  RAJESH, MARINE, VASCONEQ, TRANSRAILL, BRAHMINFRA, CAPACITE), `--parts registry,pages`, run under
  `.tmp/t232/pilot` because the worker sandbox denies D: writes (the controller runs the 394 on D:; the
  `decks403` folder keeps the ticket's name): all 40 recipe
  steps and 10 route steps rc=0, no `ram_stop`, no timeout; 58 decks and 1,838 pages, 294.3 MB fetched and 4.46 MB
  of stores; wall 1,475 s total, median 145 s (max 249 s), peak child RSS 395 MB. Every store opens with
  `company`, `doc` (deck type only) and `doc_page`, and its `routes.csv` has exactly one row per `doc_page` row
  (0 incomplete; 928 `no_figures`, 468 `table_or_chart`, 418 `needs_ocr`, 24 `text_ok`). The T-230 smoke test
  (`python -m reality_engine.v2.observations --stores-root .tmp/t232/pilot --stores d001,d003,d007 --doc-types
  deck --limit 30`) read 30 pages: 760 observations, 2 quarantined (0.3 %), 0 errors, 0 parse failures, 62 label
  proposals, 155 s.
  Sandbox note: this dsh Windows profile runs Python as an identity that cannot write into a Python 3.14
  `mkdtemp` folder (mode 0o700), and the RawStore downloads each company's PDFs into one. The pilot therefore
  ran with `PYTHONPATH` pointing at `.tmp/t232/shim`, whose `sitecustomize` creates the folder `0o777`; run the
  real build on D: outside the workspace sandbox (a normal console) and no shim is needed.

- **T-238 stage M: call transcripts and the dictionary-or-NEW measure contract (22 Nov 2026).** `v2/observations.py` reads
  transcripts and refuses silent measure invention. A reply row's measure is a dictionary id or `NEW:<snake_case>`
  WITH `new_definition` and `new_driver_node`; a bare non-dictionary id is kept but `unverified` reason
  `undeclared_new`, a NEW id without its definition/node is unverified, letterhead ids (`postal_code`, `page_number`,
  date ids) are dropped as junk, and `NEW:<id>` brackets are normalised. `measure_new` holds one row per declared id
  (counts, pages, definitions) and `<out>.new_merges.csv` holds model-free merge proposals for review (name tokens +
  definition Jaccard + unit family; nothing applied). `--doc-types transcript` reads every text page of a store's call
  transcripts from `doc_page` (not routes.csv), text-only, through `PROMPT_TRANSCRIPT`, and stores `record_type`
  `statement` (verbatim sentence, speaker, direction) and `guidance` (period, low/high, unit, direction; verifies when
  its value tokens and period words are in the printed sentence) beside the numbers. The measure-choice rule now
  spells out T-237's force-fit traps (segment slice vs total, Total Liabilities incl. equity, pre-exceptional PBT,
  cash-flow change vs stock, combined Loans & Investments, industry growth vs volume, cash profit, JV PBT, PAT to
  owners). DeepSeek V4.1 Flash is the transcript second reader (`--reader deepseek-v4.1-flash --text-only`; MODELS
  entry on the Go base, `OPENCODE_GO_API_KEY`, `reasoning_effort: "none"` is required or its reply is reasoning-only).
  Measured 22 Nov: hy4 240 transcript pages, 4,308 rows (2,190 numbers, 1,969 statements, 149 guidance), 175 s;
  DeepSeek 166 SOMANYCERA pages, 2,174 rows, 233 s; vs Opus's SOMANYCERA read hy4 reproduced 86.9% of his numbers and
  60.3% of his statements, DeepSeek 84.1% / 63.8%; on the 40 graded measure cases the new prompt keeps Opus's graded
  meaning in 28 of 40 (12 exact, 7 declared NEW equal, 9 NEW synonym) against the old 13 exact. Recheck gate on
  `.tmp/t230/obs_91_i.db`: 118 still right, 18 fixed, 0 newly broken. Tests
  `reality_engine/tests/test_observations.py` 138 passed. Details and the 40-case table: `docs/V2_SCHEMA_CONTRACT.md`
  section 17.v; artifacts `.tmp/t238/`.
- **T-238 stage O: statements as whole management passages (23 Nov 2026).** A statement is now one management
  speaker's continuous passage on one topic, verbatim from its first word to its last full stop (2-5 sentences, never

  starts); management speakers only, and a short answer's question goes in the new `observation.question_context`
  column. Every guidance row and every number row with forward or causal wording also emits its sentence as a
  statement row. Checks: `statement_cut` (no terminal punctuation or ending on and/the/of/to/in) is quarantined,
  a passage naming nothing and carrying no measure is unverified `no_subject`, and a guidance row whose value no

  6,709/1,532 tokens per page; `regress observations` on `.tmp/t230/obs_91_i.db` 118 right / 18 fixed / 0 newly
  broken; `test_observations.py` **142 passed**; typecheck clean. Details: `docs/V2_SCHEMA_CONTRACT.md` section
  17.v; scorer `.tmp/t238/score_statements.py`.

  `status_reason += 'junk_measure: <pattern>'` requiring both bare-name regex and value/unit check across

  order, British/US spelling, curated true-synonym map) to match `measure_dict` / `measure_alias`, and clusters
  remaining `NEW:*` names in `>= 3` companies within matching unit families (fixing `observations.py`'s `rs` plural
  unit-family bug via `effective_unit_family`; `%` vs currency vs days vs capacity never merge), writing
  `measure_map(new_name, canonical, source, rows)`, refreshing `measure_new` via `observations.index_new_measures`,

  Measured 24 Nov on the 394-company snapshot (`obs394.db`, `113,864` `NEW:*` rows / `69,148` distinct names,

  `identifier_or_reg` `766`, `contact_or_address` `379`, `call_time` `193`, `qa_question_count` `64`); `measure_map`

  `none` `95,451`; `NEW:*` rows `113,864 -> 112,907`, distinct `NEW:*` names `69,148 -> 67,673` (`65,600`

  unquarantined metadata `NEW:conference_call_period` and `NEW:office_number`), `20/20` quarantined junk rows truly
