- **Loader sources: T-242 reads served first; the widened deck read loads beside the primary (24-26 Nov 2026, Eagle).** `load_observations.OBS_SOURCES` entries that share a source are an ordered priority list: for each symbol the first db holding rows for it is loaded and the older dbs are skipped, so the gated T-242 reads (decks `hy4_out/obs_decks_fy27.db`, transcripts v3 `obs_transcripts_fy27_v3.db` then `obs_transcripts_394_v3.db`) replace the T-230/T-238 reads company by company, and a company hy4 has not reached keeps its older rows (annual reports stay on `t230/obs_ar_91_m.db` until an AR bulk passes). `SUPPLEMENTS` (26 Nov) loads a db BESIDE a source's primary instead of competing with it: `hy4_out/obs_decks_wide.db` (8,427 page reads, 7,566 distinct `(doc_key, page)` with observations: 87,592 verified / 64,150 unverified / 267 quarantined rows) re-read the few-figure and jumbled-text pages, and its page set holds every one of fy27's 4,185 pages, so the brief's "not in the fy27 page set" is true of the other 3,381 pages only. The loader appends only the rows on pages the primary source supplied none of, keeps `source='deck'` (the tools filter by it), marks `reader` with the db (`hy4-flash-free [deck_wide]`) and offsets `observation_id` by 10,000,000 (`SUPPLEMENTS`' 4th field; both dbs number from 1, so the offset names the wide rows in the tools' `provenance.row_id`); checks are looked up with the ORIGINAL id, so a verdict still lands on its row, and a reload deletes the offset rows first, so it is idempotent. Events: `EVENTS_PRIORITY` (round 7 since 25 Nov afternoon, see the live-gate entry; before that round 6 `t242/events_r6_fresh{4,3}.db`; round 5's `events_r5_*` copies are kept as history) then `EVENTS_DB` (t231), same rule; `filing_event` gets the union of the events dbs' columns (ALTER on old stores, e.g. `customer_desc`). Stats name the db used (`obs_db` keyed `deck` and `deck_wide`, `events_db`). Not run yet: store builds are paused (T-242); a universe filing_events run is still needed (rounds 7b/8 merged 25 Nov; fund_raise stays unverified until a fresh-store grade). Tests: `SourcePriorityTests` in `test_obs_wiring.py` and `test_load_observations.py` (4: page-disjoint add, idempotent reload, checks by original id, a symbol with no primary rows). Scratch load of 3 universe-store copies (CROMPTON, KEI, AGL): primary deck rows identical on id/doc_key/page/measure/value/period/basis/status/reader (632/495/306), +484/+116/+11 supplement rows on +112/+15/+2 pages, every supplement page absent from the primary's page set, second load unchanged.
- **hy4 reads loaded into the pilot stores; frozen copies for the frozen banks (T-242, 25 Nov 2026, Eagle; Shivam's OK ~20:40).** The 24 symbols held both by a pilot store and a universe store (the registry stays pilot-first) got their hy4 `obs_fact` reads loaded into the pilot store (`load_observations._load_store(<pilot>, <symbol>)`, plan `C:/RealityEngine/t242/pilot_obs_plan.json`): bluestar, aurobindo (moved to C:/RealityEngine/aurobindo/ the same evening, ssd_only rule; registry repointed), n10 (6), pilot10 (BAJFINANCE), p20new (8), q12 (2), u8 (5). Each pilot store was first copied to `<store>_before_obs_2026-11-25.db` beside it (the registry scanner skips `_before_`): **frozen-bank scoring runs must pin `REALITY_PILOT3_DB` to those copies** so their numbers stay comparable with earlier runs.
- **Loader re-applies the live event gate (T-242, 25 Nov 2026, Eagle).** `load_observations._gate_event_rows` loads an event row `verified` only when its kind is in the CURRENT `filing_events.VERIFIED_KINDS` (empty since 25 Nov), whatever the source db says, so the t231 fallback (263 order_win + 147 fund_raise + ... verified) and the round-6 dbs (verified order wins) never serve a failed kind as verified; stats count the rows moved (`events_ungated`). `EVENTS_PRIORITY` now = round-7 dbs `C:/RealityEngine/t242/orderwin_r7d/events_r7d_fresh{5,4,3}.db` and `fresh6/events_r7_fresh6.db`. Tests `test_load_observations_gate.py`. Shivam 25 Nov: load decks, universe transcripts and (once graded) order wins as soon as each passes.
- **Sonnet settles unverified hy4 rows (T-242, Wolf, 24 Nov, Shivam: spare Wolf quota).** `python -m reality_engine.scripts.sonnet_settle --db <hy4 obs db> --stores-root <build root> --out D:/RealityEngine/t242/sonnet_settle/<set> [--max-pages N] [--pages-per-run N] [--max-runs-per-window N]` sends one bare headless `claude -p --model sonnet --effort medium` run per 12 pages (`--pages-per-run`, default 12) with each page's text (first 12,000 chars) and its UNVERIFIED company number rows; Sonnet answers confirm / fix (period, unit, scale, basis, segment) / reject / unsure with an evidence snippet and header. Verdicts go to `<out>/verdicts.jsonl` keyed by observation_id; the hy4 DB is never written, so a bad fix rolls back by dropping the verdict. One run at a time on Wolf's config (`SONNET_SETTLE_CONFIG=C:\Users\warri\.claude` runs it on Eagle's account, Shivam 24 Nov while Wolf's window is spent). It stops with exit 0 and no sleep loop, for a person to resume: when its usage reading is older than 5 min (the file only refreshes when Wolf's interactive session prompts, so a stale read must never be trusted), when Wolf's 5-hour use is >= 75%, or when this script has already fired `--max-runs-per-window` runs (default 25) in the current 5-hour window. Each run appends `{ts, five_hour_resets_at, cost}` to `<out>/runs.jsonl` keyed by the reading's `rate_limits.five_hour.resets_at`, and only runs of the current window count. The call is bare (`--tools ""`, `--setting-sources ""`, `--strict-mcp-config` with `--mcp-config '{"mcpServers":{}}'`, cwd `<out>/bare`), so no tools, MCP servers, settings, CLAUDE.md or memory load. Test `reality_engine/tests/test_sonnet_settle.py` covers the stale exit, the 75% stop, the per-window cap and the bare command line. Deck pilot (1,265 rows): 511 confirm, 379 fix, 98 reject, 277 unsure; strict DeepSeek grade (seed 7171) confirm 29R/1P/0W, fix 29R/1P/0W, reject 9 agree / 1 disagree, so confirm/fix may be served labelled `model_checked` and reject is advisory only (never deletes a row). Not for transcript call numbers (filing match only, ruling 24 Nov). Served (24 Nov): `load_observations.CHECKS` maps a source obs db to its verdicts files; the loader adds `check_status` (`model_checked` for confirm/fix, `model_rejected`, `model_unsure`), `check_fields` (a fix's corrections, JSON), `check_evidence` and `checker` to `obs_fact` (ALTER on old stores), and `_obs_rows` returns them as `check` {status, by, evidence, corrected}; the row's own status and fields never change (test `test_model_check_is_served_labelled_and_keeps_the_row`). Open: a fix with no changed field is a confirm. (Ranges now fill `value_high` and date-shaped values are gated `date_value` in `build_observations(deck_guards=True)`; see the deck value-ranges entry below.)
- **Deck value ranges and date values (T-242, 24 Nov 2026, `v2/observations.py`).** Two number-row defects the
  strict deck grades caught, fixed in script with no prompt change (`read_gate.fingerprint('deck')` stays
  `ea5e621d2826`): (1) a printed range ("₹22–25 Crore", "22–25%", "15-16 billion") was stored with `value` = the low
  end and `value_high` empty, so a served 22 read as a point; with `deck_guards`, `deck_range_high` fills
  `value_high` with the high end (same printed scale/unit) when `value_text` holds exactly one range and the stored
  value is its low end, and notes `range:` in `status_reason` without changing the row's status. 'FY25-26',
  '2025-26' and 'Q1-Q2' are periods and '-25%' is a minus, never ranges. (2) a date/quarter/FY stored as a number
  ("Start of Production (SOP): Q1 FY28" as value 1.0 with no unit, ACC's "20 Jul'26" as 20) is not a quantity: with
  `deck_guards`, `deck_date_value` makes the row `unverified` with `date_value: a date/quarter/FY is not a quantity`
  (a bare year counts only when the row prints no unit, so "1989" in INR Mn stays a money value). Deck-only; other
  doc types are unchanged. Measured in-process, read-only, on the 4,594-row deck entity DB
  (`D:/RealityEngine/hy4_out/pilot_decks_entity.db`): (a) 11 rows gain `value_high` (4 verified, 7 unverified, e.g.
  ACCELERATE p3 `NEW:group_revenue_run_rate` `₹22–25 Crore` 22 -> 22/25, ADANIPORTS p37 `mix.share` `~25–30%`
  25 -> 25/30, AMBER p12 `ebitda_margin_change_bps` `50-100 bps` 50 -> 50/100); (b) 21 date-shaped rows, all already
  unverified, 0 verified rows turn unverified (under the 50-row stop), e.g. BELRISE p12 `Q1 FY28`, ACC p13
  `20 Jul'26`, BCONCEPTS p31 `2030`. Tests `reality_engine/tests/test_deck_guards.py` (5 new tests).
- **Annual-report fresh2 rules: net-cash sign and another reporting entity (T-242, 24 Nov 2026, `v2/observations.py`).**
  Two more script-catchable wrong rows from the fresh2 strict grade (57 right / 0 partial / 3 wrong, exactly at the
  70%-right bar). (1) net cash sign: a verified AR number row whose `printed_label` or `series_title` says "net cash"
  (not a "net cash flow"/"net cash from"/"net cash used in" line) under a `net_debt*` measure is the opposite of net
  debt and goes unverified `ar_net_cash_sign:`; e.g. ANTHEM's "Net cash / EBITDA" chart bars were stored
  `net_debt_to_ebitda`. (2) other reporting entity: when the page head (first 300 characters, the basis rule's
  window) names a company followed by "financial statements" / "Notes to" / "Balance Sheet" / "Statement of
  Profit", or the page's printed running header reads `<COMPANY> | FINANCIALS` / `| ANNUAL REPORT`, and the name is
  not the store's own company, the rows are that entity's statements: `entity_scope='subsidiary'`,
  `segment=<that company name>` and unverified `ar_other_entity:`. The own-company comparison strips a leading
  "The", reduces names to letters and digits ("A. K." == "AK") and reads a shared 12-character head as own (an
  abbreviated store name, "Bombay Dyeing & Mfg" vs "The Bombay Dyeing and Manufacturing", is the company's own
  page); a row already labelled that subsidiary keeps verified. The caller passes the store company name; without
  it rule 2 does not run. Measured in-process on `C:/RealityEngine/t242/pilot_ar_gates.db` (read-only, 200 pages /
  5,607 rows; page text from `C:/RealityEngine/tg_build/universe_fy27/cNN/cNN.db`): 5 ANTHEM net-cash rows and 6
  Blue Dart Aviation ratio rows (page 279, the W #57 ROIC among them) gated; 0 of the 57 graded R rows lose
  verified; W #10 and W #57 are caught (W #21, an undated protest payment, is outside these rules and stays
  verified). Tests: 3 new in `reality_engine/tests/test_ar_sections.py` with their counters ("Net cash flow from
  operating activities" stays verified; a page headed with the company's own name stays `company`). The
  `read_gate` annual_report fingerprint is unchanged (`48d75aa69de7`): no prompt or template touched.
- **Deck rows take their basis from a per-period page footnote (T-242, 24 Nov 2026, `v2/observations.py`).** A deck
  footer can pair each period with its own basis ("FY26 CONSOLIDATED, FY25 STANDALONE", or two lines "FY26
  Consolidated" / "FY25 Standalone"); the page-level `detect_basis` / `page_basis` read one word for the whole page,
  and `page_basis` accepts a bare "standalone" but not a bare "consolidated", so BAIKAKAJI p33 (obs 30422) stored
  the FY26 cell (11.37 Rs Cr) `basis='standalone'`. With `deck_guards`, `page_footnote_bases(page_text)` finds the
  period-basis pairings (`PAGE_BASIS_PAIR_RE`) only when two or more distinct periods are paired;
  `fiscal_year_key` normalises FY26 / FY2026 / FY 2025-26 to '26' and row periods are compared at the same annual
  granularity (a "Q1 FY26" / "H1 FY26" period is not the year and stays untouched). Each number row whose period is
  one of the paired years takes that basis with `basis_source='page_footnote'`. One pairing or none: no change (the
  existing `footnote_basis` and page rules stay). A period missing from the pairing keeps the page result.
  `--recheck` applies the same rule for deck pages, so a fresh read and a recheck agree. Measured in-process,
  read-only, on the 476 docs / 16,695 pages behind `D:/RealityEngine/hy4_out/obs_decks_fy27.db`: 51 pages print a
  period-basis pairing, 3 pair two or more; 56 of the 66,653 number rows would change basis (16 verified / 40
  unverified), under the 300-verified stop. Examples: BAIKAKAJI p33 FY26 balance-sheet rows standalone ->
  consolidated; the other two are the same template page (8562ffcf p33) and a cash-flow page with "FY26
  Consolidated" / "FY25 Standalone" on separate lines (f9e69bcc p21). No prompt or template change:
  `read_gate.fingerprint('deck')` stays `ea5e621d2826`. Tests: 3 new in
  `reality_engine/tests/test_deck_guards.py` (17 pass).
- **Deck row gates from the 60-row surprise audit (T-242, 25 Nov 2026, `reality_engine/v2/observations.py`).**
  `apply_number_gates(..., doc_type="deck")` and `recheck_db` (for `doc_type="deck"`) now call
  `apply_deck_row_gates(rows, page_text)` with three script-level gates (no prompt change;
  `read_gate.fingerprint('deck')` stays `ea5e621d2826`):
  (1) `deck_bound_reason`: marks a verified row unverified (`deck_bound_not_exact: ...`) when `value_text` or
  `printed_label` puts a true bound (`>`, `<`, `≥`, `≤`, `>=`, `<=`, `over`, `more than`, `less than`, `above`,
  `below`, `up to/upto`, `at least`, `exceeding`) right before the row's number (excluding Balance Sheet
  `"other than ... above"`, `"& above"`, and `"respectively over"`; printed approximations `~`, `nearly`,
  `approx/approximately` stay verified), catching `>10,000` (BANKBARODA) and `>100% YoY` (AEQUS);
  (2) `deck_combined_label_reason`: marks a verified row unverified (`deck_combined_label: ...`) only when BOTH
  sides of `&`, `and`, or `+` in `printed_label` match the `DECK_COMBINED_CONCEPTS` financial-measure vocabulary
  (revenue/sales, income, other income, EBITDA, EBIT, PBT, PAT, profit, margin, GNPA, NNPA, goodwill, intangibles,
  share of profit/associate/JV, ATMs, cash recyclers, branches, deposits, advances, AUM, opex, capex), the stored
  `measure` names only one side, AND the combined label carries ONE figure (skipping labels/page lines with >=2
  figures, `'respectively'`, or `X & Margin` / `X & X Margin` / `X & Growth` pairs). Keeps `'EBITDA and PAT margins
  for Q2 stood at 22.1% / 10.5%'`, `'GNPA & NNPA stood at 0.29% and 0.12%'`, `'EBIT Margin & PBT Margin ... 8.0%'`,
  and `'EBITDA & EBITDA Margin'`; catches `'Other income & share of associate'`, `'Goodwill & Intangibles Assets'`,
  `'ATMs and cash recyclers'`, and `'Revenue + Other Comprehensive Income'`;
  (3) `heading_basis(page_text, SECTION_BASIS_RE)` (shared helper extracted from `apply_ar_row_gates`): fills an empty
  `basis` with `basis_source='page_heading'` when the first 300 characters of the page name exactly one of
  `Consolidated` or `Standalone` (fixing BLUESTONE p40 `'Consolidated | INR mn'`); the ALLTIME p12 starred-column
  case (`H1FY27*` / `*Consolidated`) is left untouched and documented as known.
  Measured read-only on a SQLite backup copy of `D:/RealityEngine/hy4_out/obs_decks_fy27.db` (66,526 verified number
  rows, `REALITY_RAW_FETCH=0`, pages from `C:/RealityEngine/tg_build/universe_fy27`): Rule 1 changes 81 (0.12%),
  Rule 2 changes 11 (0.02%), Rule 3 fills 242 (0.36%), all under the 4% stop bar. Tests: 4 new tests in
  `reality_engine/tests/test_deck_guards.py` (21 pass).
- **Results row gates: non-figures, entity scope, holding/fragment values, the unit line (T-242, 25 Nov 2026,
  `reality_engine/v2/observations.py`).** The first strict 60-row results grade
  (`D:/RealityEngine/t242/grades/results_pilot_b9dfba35.md`, `D:/RealityEngine/hy4_out/obs_results_pilot.db`) was
  33 R / 12 P / 6 W / 9 J: years and serials from results notes stored as figures, the company's own cash flow
  tagged subsidiary, an associate's PAT and another company's holding stored as the company's, a value read off a
  longer printed figure, and the "Rs in lakhs" line lost. `apply_results_row_gates(rows, page_text, company,
  previous_text, stats)` now runs for `doc_type="results"` inside `apply_number_gates` and in `recheck_db` (no
  prompt change): (1) `results_junk_reason` sets `quarantined` for a date printed as the value
  (`RESULTS_DATE_TEXT_RE`), a time, a whole year 1900-2100 with no unit that the row's own period/label prints as a
  date or whose measure is a reference (`RESULTS_REFERENCE_MEASURE_RE`: statutory/legal/circular/reference year), a
  section/rule/regulation/scrip-code serial (unit empty, integer printed in the label beside a reference word), or
  a percent that only repeats a non-growth condition in its own label (a `share`/`margin`/`ratio`/`growth`/`pct`
  measure and an `AR_CHANGE_WORD_RE` label are spared); (2) `results_scale_note` reads the page's printed scale
  line, bracketed or bare and with an OCR'd scale ("Figures~ ln l akhs", "(Rs. in lakhs, unless otherwise
  stated)", "(₹ in lacs)"), and an empty unit or a misspelled scale word ("l akhs", "(Rupees in Lakll )") takes
  it — the page's line first, else the same line on the three pages before it (`previous_pages_text`), a known
  unit is never replaced, and date/quarter-end measures are skipped; (3) `_results_scope_reset` resets
  `entity_scope='subsidiary'` to `company` when the page head begins with the store company's name and the row's
  series/page names its own cash-flow/balance-sheet statement (unless `_ar_keep_subsidiary_scope` protects a named
  subsidiary table), while `_results_associate_reason` marks an associate/JV figure (its group/series names the
  associate) unverified when the label does not itself name the associate line; (4) `_results_holding_reason` marks
  a `count.shares` row whose value's sentence is an equity stake/holding in another company unverified, and
  `_results_fragment_reason` marks a bare integer that is only the start of the longer figure printed right after
  its own label ("5" for "55.43"). Measured on a copy of the pilot DB (`REALITY_RAW_FETCH=0`, 188 pages from the
  145 stores that hold its documents, `--recheck --doc-types results`; `.tmp/results_recheck5.db`): 5,430 rows,
  verified 1,477 -> 1,421; junk 175 rows (55 of them verified, 3.72%), unit 715 (119 verified, 8.06%), scope reset
  200, associate 1, holding 2, fragment 1 — every rule under the 10% verified stop bar. Of the 60 graded rows, 0 R
  changed: the 9 J are quarantined, 33/51/60 unverified, 5/6/27 now `company`, and the 7 lakh rows
  (1,3,10,16,25,35,43) carry `Lakhs`/scale 1e5. Tests: `reality_engine/tests/test_results_gates.py` (16 pass); the
  per-rule and 60-row samples are in `tasks/t242_results_measure.md`.

- **Results row gates r2: cash-flow measure kind, basis, period duration, scale inheritance, JV/associate scope
  (T-242, 26 Nov 2026, `reality_engine/v2/observations.py`).** The results ckpt_0001 strict grade
  (`D:/RealityEngine/hy4_out/obs_results_fy27.db.audit/ckpt_0001.fix.md`,
  `C:/RealityEngine/t242/grades/results_ckpt0001_3639e519.md`) failed 40 R / 20 P / 0 W / 0 J: every P was a right
  value with a weak attribute. `apply_results_row_gates(rows, page_text, company, previous_text, stats,
  previous_page_text, doc_note, doc_dual_statement)` adds, for results pages only (no prompt change): (1) a
  cash-flow statement line (page or the one page before headed "cash flow") that ends in "paid", or carries
  "Increase/(Decrease) in", "(Increase)/Decrease in", "Proceeds from", "Repayment(s)" or "Payment of" never keeps
  the P&L / balance-sheet measure of the stock it moves: it takes the dictionary's cash-flow measure when one
  names the line (`direct_taxes_paid`, `cash_generated_from_operations`, `changes_in_working_capital`), else a
  `NEW:<line>_cash_flow` id and `unverified` (`results_cash_flow_measure`); a row already carrying a cash-flow
  measure, an `changes_in_*` measure or a `NEW:` id that names the flow is untouched. (2) `basis`: the page's one
  `standalone`/`consolidated` word sets it (`basis_source='page_heading'`), else the one page before carries it
  (`previous_page_heading`); `_results_doc_context` scans each document's `doc_page` once (cached) for its single
  printed scale and for both a `Standalone …` and a `Consolidated …` statement heading, and when the document
  prints both statements while neither page names one, a verified dictionary-measure row is `unverified`
  (`basis_unstated`; a `NEW:` measure is left alone). (3) `period`: a bare year ("2026") or a release/press date
  ("May 13, 2026") as the row's period is `results_period_not_a_period`; when one end date is printed under two
  durations ("quarter ended …" and "year to date … ended") and the row stores the bare date,
  `results_period_duration_missing`. (4) `unit`: a currency-only unit ("Rs.", "₹") whose scale is 1 takes the
  page's scale line, else the three pages before it, else the document's one printed scale (`results_unit`,
  `results_doc_units`); a per-share measure (EPS, DPS, book value / face value per share) never takes a money
  scale — the currency is kept and `scale` reset to 1 (`results_per_share_unit`). (5) `entity`: a "share of (net)
  profit/loss … joint venture/associate" line is that entity's figure (`entity_scope` jv/associate; the pipeline's
  `gate_not_company` holds an unnamed one unverified) and a share of loss printed unsigned is stored negative
  (`results_share_of_loss_sign`); a business the page says is "not consolidated … equity accounting" is the
  associate's figure, `basis` cleared, unverified (`results_equity_accounted`).
  Measured with a scratch script calling the same gate over the 49,993 rows of a copy of `obs_results_fy27.db` and
  the stores' `doc_page` texts (1,858 pages, no model, no fetch): verified 14,171; 1,201 verified rows become
  unverified across all five rules, no single rule over the 3% stop: `basis_unstated` 380 (2.68%), period
  duration 281 (1.98%), bare year/release date 239 (1.69%), cash-flow lines 277 (1.95%), equity-accounted 24;
  625 cash-flow lines are remapped in all (348 were already unverified) and 291 per-share rows lose their money
  scale with status unchanged. The 40 graded-R rows of `results_ckpt1` keep their status (row 42 only gains
  `basis='standalone'`). The 20 P rows: 2/16/25 `basis_unstated`, 30/36/37/43 cash-flow `NEW:` + unverified,
  31 bare year, 33 basis + release date, 35 associate + basis cleared, 46 EPS unit `₹`, 59 duration, 60 jv with
  value −8,193.50; 8/48/51/52 now carry their basis (48/51/52 stay verified; 52 also Lakhs), 11 Lakhs; 24 (the
  misaligned OCR row) and 26 (its page prints no duration word at all) are unchanged. A full `--recheck` of a copy
  was started but spent > 25 min loading page words/PDFs and was abandoned; the gate path is the same function.
  Tests: `reality_engine/tests/test_results_gates.py` 26 pass (13 new); the four acceptance files 275 pass. Under
  the Windows sandbox pytest's 0o700 temp dirs are unlistable, so the run used `.tmp/pt_tmp_plugin.py`
  (`-p pt_tmp_plugin`, `T242_TMPROOT` a workspace dir) as a test-runner-only tmp factory; the acceptance commands
  themselves error at `tmp_path` setup there for the 5 tests that use it.

- **AR row gates: non-figures and outside entities (T-242, 25 Nov 2026, Wolf).** AR bulk ckpt_0001 failed 51/5/1/3 (Sonnet x2; W+J 6.7%): a GRI index page number, a clause '(1)', the 3 of 'in the past 3 years', and an industry 2030 projection were verified. `apply_ar_row_gates` now marks a verified AR number unverified when its measure names a reference number (`AR_REFERENCE_MEASURE_RE`: page_ref, clause, note/section/regulation/reference number, serial no) -> `ar_not_a_figure`; when the value only repeats its label's duration ('past/last N years', `_label_duration_echo`) -> `ar_not_a_figure`; or when the row's entity_scope is industry, country or peer (`AR_OUTSIDE_SCOPES`; customer left alone) -> `ar_outside_entity`. Rows stay served, labelled. On obs_ar_fy27.db: 39 + 259 of 11,086 verified (2.7%); a 14-row sample of the outside rows were all outside figures (IMF growth, repo rate, industry output). Test `test_ar_ckpt_0001_non_figures_and_outside_entities_are_unverified` (keeps a portfolio fee, a customer split and a 3-year dividend total). `--recheck --stores-root` in `reality_engine/v2/observations.py` now loads every `cNN/cNN.db` under the root (like `--reparse`, instead of `seed_stores`'s default 5-store `--sample`) and `recheck_db` counts skipped pages in `stats['no_store']`, so rechecking an AR DB reaches every store and applies `apply_ar_row_gates` (`test_recheck_db_applies_ar_row_gates_to_verified_ar_rows`, `test_recheck_cli_stores_root_scans_every_store_beyond_default_sample`). `_label_duration_echo` also catches `'N years (immediately) preceding/before/prior/ago'` and `'period of N years'` (digits or number words across `printed_label`, `period`, `measure`), and `_is_target_year` marks a verified whole year 1990-2100 with no unit (or unit `'year'`) and a `target`/`by`/`goal`/`net zero`/`year` cue in `printed_label` or `measure` as `ar_not_a_figure` while keeping 2040 counts/money with their units (`test_ar_duration_echo_variants_and_target_year_are_unverified`).
- **AR row gates: ckpt_0002 periods, date values, prose consolidated basis, consolidated subsidiary scope (T-242, 25 Nov 2026, `reality_engine/v2/observations.py`).** AR bulk ckpt_0002 failed 53/3/3/1 (W+J 6.7%). Four script-only rules added inside `apply_ar_row_gates` (plus `recheck_db` deleting dropped rows, AR prompt fingerprint unchanged at `48d75aa69de7`): (1) `ar_report_fy` / `_parse_ar_fy_spec` extracts the AR's fiscal period from explicit `year ended <date>` headers on pages 1-10 (e.g. `ABB` -> `'CY2025'`, `ACCELYA` -> `'Year ended 30 June 2026'`) or split years (`2025-26`, `2025-2026`, `FY26`, `FY 2025-26`) on title/cover/page heads, leaving bare single-year titles (e.g. `AMBALALSA`) empty; `_ar_period_reason` resolves relative AR periods accordingly (`'FY2025-26'`/`'31 March 2026'`/`'1 April 2025'`/`'FY2024-25'` for March year-ends, `'CY2025'`/`'31 December 2025'`/`'1 January 2025'`/`'CY2024'` for December year-ends, `'Year ended DD Month YYYY'`/`'1 <NextMonth> YYYY-1'` for other non-March year-ends) with `period_source='ar_report_year'` and keeps status, leaves bare single-year ARs and non-calendar phrases untouched (never `period_no_year`), and marks `unverified` (`period_not_printed`) when an explicit year/FY is printed on neither the page nor the previous page (`#46 AFSL` `'December 2019'`, `#49 63MOONS` `'March 2022'`); (2) `_is_ar_date_value` drops (`junk`) any AR number row whose `value_text` parses as a calendar date (`Month DD, YYYY`, `DD Month YYYY`, `Month YYYY`, `dd.mm.yyyy`, `dd/mm/yyyy`, `yyyy-mm-dd`, e.g. `#13 ARL` `'December 01, 2026'`), whatever the measure; (3) `basis='consolidated'` set from running prose or `'the Group'` without a heading/table-title line (`_ar_has_consolidated_heading`, `_ar_is_consolidated_heading_line`) or `page_heading`/`previous_page_heading` `basis_source` is cleared (`#39 5PAISA`); (4) `entity_scope='subsidiary'` on plain consolidated items under a Consolidated/Group first-300-char heading (`AR_CONS_GROUP_HEAD_RE`) resets to `'company'` (`#21 BALKRISIND`), keeping Schedule III `'Additional Information'`/`'share in net assets'` tables, NCI/stake/associate/JV/`'held ...% in'` labels, and proper company names (`_ar_keep_subsidiary_scope`). ### P. Checkpoint grading packs: one command, two rubrics (T-242, 25 Nov 2026)

`python -m reality_engine.scripts.grade_pack` is the repo version of the ad-hoc `C:/RealityEngine/t242/packs/mkpack.py`. It takes every verified row of `--record-type number|statement|guidance` named by the checkpoint, fills to `--size` (default 60) from the observation DB's verified rows of that type with `--seed` (a new symbol first, at most two rows per document), and writes each row with `page_text`, `previous_page_text` and `in_checkpoint`; `<out>_part00.jsonl`, `<out>_part01.jsonl` hold the `--split`-row halves (default 30) for two graders. Store DBs come from `--stores-glob` (skipping `*pages.db` and `*_ocr.db`) and are mapped through `doc_page.doc_key`; every DB is opened read-only. It prints the row count, how many came from the checkpoint and how many pages are missing.

```bash
python -m reality_engine.scripts.grade_pack --db <observation db> --ckpt <ckpt.jsonl> \
    --stores-glob "C:/RealityEngine/tg_build/full394/*/*.db" --record-type statement \
    --seed 7 --out C:/RealityEngine/t242/packs/v3_ckpt6.jsonl
```

Graders score the halves with `docs/grading/statement_rubric.md` (R/P/W/J plus the `qc` column that checks `question_context` against the printed analyst question) and `docs/grading/number_rubric.md` (R/P/W/J for deck/AR numbers: the printed value, the company's own, measure/period/unit/scale/basis right; J for page/note/clause numbers, years and label-duration echoes). Tests: `python -m pytest -q reality_engine/tests/test_grade_pack.py` -> 4 passed.

## Skills catalog and rule hooks (T-243, Shivam 25 Nov 2026)

Repeatable workflows live as project skills in `.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` (tracked in git, seen by Wolf and Eagle; call by name or let the description trigger them). Each is a short, exact recipe: inputs, commands, accept bar, pitfalls from past failures. Update a skill in the same change as the workflow it describes.

| Skill | Use |
|---|---|
| `free-model-bakeoff` | choose a free model for a reading/OCR job; gate each on one call; never call paid ids |
| `checkpoint-grade` | 60-row non-author grade of a reader checkpoint; `read_gate` record; fix.md on fail |
| `hy4-store-load` | recheck on a copy, verify, swap with backup, `load_symbols`, frozen copies for frozen banks |
| `retrieval-audit` | 48 fresh questions, in-process lookups, R/P/W/M/N, comparison with earlier audits |
| `worker-brief-merge` | prescriptive brief template, review, merge recipe, review row, `By:` line |
| `row-contract` | fields every number / statement / guidance row must carry and the checks behind them |

Standing rules are enforced in two layers:
- `reality_engine/scripts/rule_guard.py`, a **PreToolUse** hook in `.claude/settings.json` (Bash, PowerShell, Read, Grep, Glob, Edit, Write): blocks (exit 2) the private Manual Dumps folder, `Downloads\Telegram Desktop`, reading credential files or printing `*KEY*/*TOKEN*` variables, `git push` of anything but an explicit `main` (dry runs pass), and paid model ids (DeepInfra, Zen MiMo/hy4, paid Kilo/OpenRouter routes), and killing processes by name (taskkill /IM, Stop-Process -Name, pkill/killall of python, node, bash or claude): on 25 Nov 21:14 a helper's kill-by-name of python took down 3 workers, the MCP servers and every uploader of both accounts; kill by PID only. Text written through a `cat`/`tee` heredoc is not scanned (it is data); interpreter heredocs are. A guard error never blocks. `--explain` lists the rules. Replayed on 9,855 past calls: 1 block, a real Telegram Desktop access; 0 false blocks. Tests: `reality_engine/tests/test_rule_guard.py`.
- `ops_gate.py` (Stop hook) keeps the state rules that a single call cannot show: DB on the HDD, two hy4 writers, heartbeat, idle workers, quota pace, worker limit.
